By allowing abstract knowledge to directly shape policy optimization, NSER achieves consistent superior sample efficiency and convergence speed across reactive, rule-based, and procedural benchmarks.

</details>


### [113] [SimWorld Studio: Automatic Environment Generation with Evolving Coding Agent for Embodied Agent Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09423)
*Haoqiang Kang,Xiaokang Ye,Yuhan Liu,Siddhant Hitesh Mantri,Lingjun Mao,James Fleming,Drishti Regmi,Lianhui Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SimWorld Studio，一个基于Unreal Engine 5的开源平台，通过SimCoder代码智能体从语言/图像指令自动生成可演化的3D具身学习环境，并支持环境生成与具身学习的协同进化，在具身导航任务中取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体缺乏丰富、多样且可自动生成的3D交互训练环境，现有模拟器依赖手工场景或程序模板，而LLM-based 3D生成系统主要生成静态场景，无法提供可部署的、带有可验证任务和标准学习接口的环境。

Method: SimWorld Studio核心是SimCoder——一个工具/技能增强的代码智能体，它编写并执行引擎级代码以从语言/图像指令构建物理合理的3D世界。SimCoder通过验证器反馈（编译错误、物理检查、VLM批评）自我进化，自动将可复用工具和技能添加到库中。生成的环境导出为Gym风格环境。平台还支持环境生成与具身学习的协同进化：智能体性能反馈引导SimCoder生成自适应课程。

Result: 三个具身导航案例研究表明：自我进化提升了生成可靠性；生成的环境显著提升了具身智能体性能，且泛化到未见过的基准测试；协同进化相比固定环境学习获得18个成功率的提升，相比未训练智能体获得40个点的提升。

Conclusion: SimWorld Studio通过代码驱动的环境生成与协同进化机制，有效解决了具身智能体训练环境稀缺的问题，验证了环境自动生成与具身学习相互促进的可行性。

Abstract: LLM/VLM-based digital agents have advanced rapidly thanks to scalable sandboxes for coding, web navigation, and computer use, which provide rich interactive training grounds. In contrast, embodied agents still lack abundant, diverse, and automatically generated 3D environments for interactive learning. Existing embodied simulators rely on manually crafted scenes or procedural templates, while recent LLM-based 3D generation systems mainly produce static scenes rather than deployable environments with verifiable tasks and standard learning interfaces. We introduce SimWorld Studio, an open-source platform built on Unreal Engine 5 for generating evolving embodied learning environments. At its core is SimCoder, a tool/skill-augmented coding agent that writes and executes engine-level code to construct physically grounded 3D worlds from language/image instructions. SimCoder self-evolves by using verifier feedback (e.g., compilation errors, physics checks, VLM critiques) to revise environments and autonomously add reusable tools and skills to its library. Generated worlds are exported as Gym-style environments for embodied agent learning. SimWorld Studio further enables co-evolution between environment generation and embodied learning: agent performance feedback guides SimCoder to generate adaptive curricula near the learner's capability frontier, so that environments become increasingly challenging as the embodied agent improves. Three case studies on embodied navigation show that self-evolution improves generation reliability, generated environments substantially improve embodied agent performance that generalizes to unseen benchmarks, and co-evolution yields an 18-point success-rate gain over fixed-environment learning and a 40-point gain over an untrained agent.

</details>


### [114] [VulTriage: Triple-Path Context Augmentation for LLM-Based Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09461)
*Wenxin Tang,Xiang Zhang,Junliang Liu,Jingyu Xiao,Xi Xiao,Jinlong Yang,Yuehe Ma,Zhenyu Liu,Zhengheng Li,Zicheng Wang,Wang Luo,Qing Li,Lei Wang,Peng Xiangli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VulTriage提出了一种三路径上下文增强框架，通过控制路径（提取AST、CFG、DFG）、知识路径（检索CWE漏洞模式）和语义路径（总结代码功能）来增强LLM的漏洞检测能力，在PrimeVul和Kotlin数据集上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的漏洞检测方法难以捕获结构依赖、领域特定的漏洞知识以及复杂的程序语义；直接使用LLM对原始源码进行检测容易漏报或误报，尤其在脆弱函数与正常函数仅有细微语义差异时。

Method: 提出VulTriage三路径上下文增强框架：1) 控制路径：提取并文本化AST、CFG、DFG信息以暴露控制流和数据依赖；2) 知识路径：通过混合稠密-稀疏检索获取CWE相关的漏洞模式和示例；3) 语义路径：在最终判断前总结代码的功能行为。将三路上下文整合为统一指令，引导LLM进行更可靠的漏洞推理。

Result: 在PrimeVul pair测试集上，VulTriage达到SOTA性能，在成对和分类指标上均优于现有深度学习和LLM基线。消融实验验证了每条路径的有效性，在Kotlin数据集上的实验证明了其在低资源和类别不平衡场景下的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 三路径上下文增强能有效提升LLM的漏洞检测准确性，且具有良好的泛化性和鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated vulnerability detection is a fundamental task in software security, yet existing learning-based methods still struggle to capture the structural dependencies, domain-specific vulnerability knowledge, and complex program semantics required for accurate detection. Recent Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong code understanding ability, but directly prompting them with raw source code often leads to missed vulnerabilities or false alarms, especially when vulnerable and benign functions differ only in subtle semantic details. To address this, we propose VulTriage, a triple-path context augmentation framework for LLM-based vulnerability detection. VulTriage enhances the LLM input through three complementary paths: a Control Path that extracts and verbalizes AST, CFG, and DFG information to expose control and data dependencies; a Knowledge Path that retrieves relevant CWE-derived vulnerability patterns and examples through hybrid dense--sparse retrieval; and a Semantic Path that summarizes the functional behavior of the code before the final judgment. These contexts are integrated into a unified instruction to guide the LLM toward more reliable vulnerability reasoning. Experiments on the PrimeVul pair test set show that VulTriage achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming existing deep learning and LLM-based baselines on key pair-wise and classification metrics. Further ablation studies verify the effectiveness of each path, and additional experiments on the Kotlin dataset demonstrate the generalization ability of VulTriage under low-resource and class-imbalanced settings. Our code is available at https://github.com/vinsontang1/VulTriage

</details>


### [115] [Don't Click That: Teaching Web Agents to Resist Deceptive Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09497)
*Yilin Zhang,Yingkai Hua,Chunyu Wei,Xin Wang,Yueguo Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DUDE框架，通过混合奖励学习与不对称惩罚机制，结合经验总结将失败模式转化为可迁移指导，在RUC基准测试上将VLM网页代理对欺骗性UI元素的敏感性降低53.8%，同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM网页代理虽能自主交互，但易受欺骗性界面元素影响；已有工作要么检测欺骗但不与任务集成，要么记录攻击而不提出防御方案，缺乏系统性的防御框架。

Method: 提出DUDE两阶段框架：1) 混合奖励学习与不对称惩罚机制进行训练；2) 经验总结模块将失败模式蒸馏为可迁移的指导信号，用于防御欺骗性UI元素。

Result: 在提出的RUC基准测试（涵盖四个领域、1,407个场景）上，DUDE将欺骗敏感性降低53.8%，且不牺牲任务完成性能。

Conclusion: DUDE为构建鲁棒的、对欺骗性界面具有防御能力的VLM网页代理奠定了有效基础，填补了欺骗感知防御的系统性空白。

Abstract: Vision-language model (VLM) based web agents demonstrate impressive autonomous GUI interaction but remain vulnerable to deceptive interface elements. Existing approaches either detect deception without task integration or document attacks without proposing defenses. We formalize deception-aware web agent defense and propose DUDE (Deceptive UI Detector & Evaluator), a two-stage framework combining hybrid-reward learning with asymmetric penalties and experience summarization to distill failure patterns into transferable guidance. We introduce RUC (Real UI Clickboxes), a benchmark of 1,407 scenarios spanning four domains and deception categories. Experiments show DUDE reduces deception susceptibility by 53.8% while maintaining task performance, establishing an effective foundation for robust web agent deployment.

</details>


### [116] [EpiGraph: A Knowledge Graph and Benchmark for Evidence-Intensive Reasoning in Epilepsy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09505)
*Yuyang Dai,Zheng Chen,Jathurshan Pradeepkumar,Yasuko Matsubara,Jimeng Sun,Yasushi Sakurai,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了大规模癫痫知识图谱EpiGraph（含48,166篇论文、24,324个实体、32,009个三元组）及五项临床推理任务的基准EpiBench，实验发现集成该知识图谱可使LLM在药物基因组推理等任务上提升30-41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫的诊断和治疗需要整合异构临床知识（生物信号、遗传机制、药物基因组学、治疗方案等），现有LLM缺乏结构化的证据密集型推理能力，因此需要构建专门的癫痫知识图谱来支撑知识增强的临床推理评估。

Method: 从48,166篇同行评审论文和7个临床资源中构建异质知识图谱EpiGraph，包含24,324个实体和32,009个证据支持的三元组，覆盖5个临床层次。基于该图设计5项临床任务，在标准设置和Graph-RAG设置下评估6种LLM。

Result: 集成EpiGraph在所有任务上均持续提升性能，其中药物基因组学推理提升最大（+30-41%），验证了结构化癫痫知识对证据驱动临床推理的增强效果。

Conclusion: 结构化癫痫知识显著增强证据驱动的临床推理能力，EpiGraph/EpiBench为知识增强LLM在真实神经学场景中的评估提供了实用基准框架。

Abstract: Epilepsy diagnosis and treatment require evidence-intensive reasoning across heterogeneous clinical knowledge, including biosignal patterns, genetic mechanisms, pharmacogenomics, treatment strategies, and patient outcomes. In this work, we present \textsc{EpiGraph}, a large-scale epilepsy knowledge graph and benchmark for evaluating knowledge-augmented clinical reasoning. \textsc{EpiGraph} integrates 48,166 peer-reviewed papers and seven clinical resources into a heterogeneous graph containing 24,324 entities and 32,009 evidence-grounded triplets across five clinical layers. Built upon this graph, \textsc{EpiBench} defines five clinically motivated tasks spanning clinical decision-making, EEG report generation, pharmacogenomic precision medicine, treatment recommendation, and deep research planning. We evaluate six LLMs under both standard and Graph-RAG settings. Results show that integrating \textsc{EpiGraph} consistently improves performance across all tasks, with the largest gains observed in pharmacogenomic reasoning (+30--41\%). Our findings demonstrate that structured epilepsy knowledge substantially enhances evidence-grounded clinical reasoning and provides a practical benchmark framework for evaluating knowledge-augmented LLMs in real-world neurological settings. Our code is available at: https://github.com/LabRAI/EEG-KG.

</details>


### [117] [WindINR: Latent-State INR for Fast Local Wind Query and Correction in Complex Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09511)
*Yi Xiao,Qilong Jia,Hang Fan,Pascal Fua,Robert Jenssen,Xiaosong Ma,Wei Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WindINR是一种隐式神经表示框架，利用稀疏观测数据实现复杂地形下连续高分辨率风场快速估算与在线校正，仅更新压缩的潜状态而非整个网络，在CPU上相比全网络微调实现约2.6倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂地形下许多下游决策需要在特定位置和高度快速获取风估计，而非固定网格上的密集预报场。现有方法无法同时满足高分辨率查询、稀疏观测校正与快速推理的需求。

Method: 提出WindINR框架，包含三阶段：1) 特权编码器从高分辨率监督中学习参考潜状态；2) 可部署潜预测器从推理输入估计初始潜状态；3) 推理时仅更新潜状态（网络权重固定），通过最小化正则化校正目标函数融合稀疏观测及其不确定性。

Result: 在Senja地区的OSS实验中，WindINR通过仅更新紧凑潜状态而非全网络即可提升局部高分辨率风估计，支持任意坐标连续查询，CPU基准测试中在线校正速度比全网络微调快约2.6倍。

Conclusion: WindINR为公里级背景产品、稀疏局部观测与复杂地形风查询之间提供了实用的接口，实现了高效且准确的局部风场校正与查询。

Abstract: Many downstream decisions in complex terrain require fast wind estimates at a small number of user-specified locations and heights for a given forecast valid time, rather than another dense forecast field on a fixed grid. We present WindINR, a latent-state implicit neural representation framework for continuous high-resolution local wind query and sparse-observation correction. WindINR maps static terrain descriptors, a low-resolution background field, and continuous query coordinates to a high-resolution wind state through a latent-conditioned decoder. To enable rapid inference-time correction, WindINR separates reusable representation learning from sample-specific latent-state correction. During training, a privileged encoder infers a reference latent state from high-resolution supervision, a deployable latent predictor estimates an initial latent state from inference-time inputs alone, and their discrepancies are summarized into a dataset-adaptive Gaussian prior over latent corrections. At inference time, within the WindINR module, network weights remain fixed and only the latent state is updated by minimizing a regularized correction objective using sparse observations and their uncertainty. In controlled OSSEs over the Senja region, including a UAV-aided approach scenario and random-observation robustness tests, WindINR improves local high-resolution wind estimates by updating only a compact latent state rather than the full network. The corrected representation remains continuously queryable at arbitrary coordinates and, in our CPU benchmark, yields about a $2.6\times$ online-correction speedup over full-network fine-tuning, suggesting a practical interface between kilometer-scale background products, sparse local observations, and wind queries in complex terrain.

</details>


### [118] [A Game Theoretic Free Energy Analysis of Higher Order Synergy in Attention Heads of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09515)
*Djamel Bouchaffra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文运用博弈论自由能原理（GTFEP）将注意力头视为有限理性智能体，通过变分推断框架分析多头注意力机制。研究发现三元交互信息为负（高阶冗余），并基于Nash均衡对应关系实现了低性能损失的头剪枝（如GPT2剪枝20%头，FLOPs降低18%，吞吐量提升22%，困惑度仅从28.4升至33.4）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头注意力机制中头与头之间的交互关系尚不明确，缺乏理论框架来理解其集体行为。

Method: 将GTFEP应用于注意力头分析：每个头最小化变分自由能，集体行为服从Gibbs分布；通过均匀先验和确定性动力学近似，将 coalition 自由能简化为联合Shannon熵；利用Harsanyi红利分解交互信息。

Result: 三元红利（交互信息）在BERT、GPT2、Llama上均为负值，表明存在高阶冗余；基于边际贡献剪枝可在极小性能损失下降低计算成本（GPT2剪枝20%头，FLOPs降18%，吞吐量升22%，困惑度仅从28.4升至33.4）。

Conclusion: GTFEP为分析和优化Transformer架构提供了 principled 的理论基础。

Abstract: Large language models rely on multihead attention, but interactions among heads remain poorly understood. We apply the Game Theoretic Free Energy Principle (GTFEP): a framework casting multiagent systems as distributed variational inference to analyze attention heads as bounded rational agents. According to GTFEP, each head minimizes its variational free energy, and collective behavior follows a Gibbs distribution over coalition structures whose energy is decomposed into Harsanyi dividends. Using a tractable approximation (uniform prior, deterministic dynamics), coalition free energy reduces to joint Shannon entropy of discretized head outputs (argmax key index). Pairwise dividends become mutual information (nonnegative), while triple dividends correspond to interaction information and can be negative. On BERT, GPT2, and Llama with GSM8K, triple dividends are consistently negative, revealing higher order redundancy. The Nash FEP correspondence guarantees that stationary points of collective free energy are epsilon Nash equilibria; thus, heads with negligible contribution can be pruned with minimal performance loss. Pruning heads with low marginal contribution reduces computational cost with minimal performance loss: for example, pruning 20% of heads in GPT2 reduces FLOPs by 18%, increases throughput by 22%, and raises perplexity only modestly (from 28.4 to 33.4 on GSM8K). Our work shows GTFEP provides a principled foundation for analyzing and optimizing transformer architectures.

</details>


### [119] [Weighted Rules under the Stable Model Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09519)
*Joohyung Lee,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在稳定模型语义下引入加权规则概念，借鉴马尔可夫逻辑的对数线性模型，为回答集程序提供概率化扩展，支持不一致解决、模型排序、概率关联和统计推理，并与ASP、马尔可夫逻辑、ProbLog、P-log等形式化方法进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 克服稳定模型语义的确定性本质，使其能够处理不一致性、模型排序、概率关联等问题，拓展回答集程序的应用范围。

Method: 借鉴马尔可夫逻辑的对数线性模型，在稳定模型语义下引入加权规则概念，将权重与规则关联，实现概率化推理。

Result: 提出了加权稳定模型的形式化框架，能够解决不一致性、排序稳定模型、关联概率，并支持统计推理；与ASP、马尔可夫逻辑、ProbLog、P-log等进行了比较。

Conclusion: 加权规则方法有效扩展了稳定模型语义的表达能力，在多个方面优于或补充了现有概率逻辑形式化方法。

Abstract: We introduce the concept of weighted rules under the stable model semantics following the log-linear models of Markov Logic. This provides versatile methods to overcome the deterministic nature of the stable model semantics, such as resolving inconsistencies in answer set programs, ranking stable models, associating probability to stable models, and applying statistical inference to computing weighted stable models. We also present formal comparisons with related formalisms, such as answer set programs, Markov Logic, ProbLog, and P-log.

</details>


### [120] [Functional Stable Model Semantics and Answer Set Programming Modulo Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09524)
*Michael Bartholomew,Joohyung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了内涵函数在回答集编程中的应用，证明了功能稳定模型语义在"模理论回答集编程"（ASPMT）框架中的重要作用，并展示了紧ASPMT程序可以转化为SMT实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将内涵函数引入回答集编程的兴趣日益增长，但现有方法中函数的角色受到限制，需要更深入的理论框架来统一和解释这些方法。

Method: 通过证明功能稳定模型语义在ASPMT框架中的核心地位，展示现有集成方法可视为其特例，并建立紧ASPMT程序到SMT实例的转换关系。

Result: 证明了紧ASPMT程序可以转化为SMT实例，类似于ASP与SAT之间的已知关系。

Conclusion: 内涵函数的引入和ASPMT框架为回答集编程提供了更统一、更强大的理论基础，拓展了其表达能力。

Abstract: Recently there has been an increasing interest in incorporating ``intensional'' functions in answer set programming. Intensional functions are those whose values can be described by other functions and predicates, rather than being pre-defined as in the standard answer set programming. We demonstrate that the functional stable model semantics plays an important role in the framework of ``Answer Set Programming Modulo Theories (ASPMT)'' -- a tight integration of answer set programming and satisfiability modulo theories, under which existing integration approaches can be viewed as special cases where the role of functions is limited. We show that ``tight'' ASPMT programs can be translated into SMT instances, which is similar to the known relationship between ASP and SAT.

</details>


### [121] [Cplus2ASP: Computing Action Language C+ in Answer Set Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09528)
*Joseph Babb,Joohyung Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Cplus2ASP系统的第二版，它实现了动作语言C+的确定性子集。系统与Causal Calculator V2完全兼容，但通过现代答案集求解技术大幅提升了速度。系统采用增量执行模式，将C+描述转换为iclingo的输入语言，并支持外部原子、用户交互模式以及多模态翻译扩展（如B和BC语言）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升动作语言C+推理系统的性能，利用现代答案集求解技术取代旧有的Causal Calculator，同时保持与Causal Calculator V2的完全兼容性。

Method: 系统将多个理论成果组合成一个翻译流水线，包括f2lp、clingo、iclingo和as2transition。在增量执行模式下，利用iclingo的增量接地机制，并通过扩展的模定理（支持嵌套表达式）来保证正确性。

Result: 新系统Cplus2ASP V2相比Causal Calculator V2显著更快，同时完全兼容其输入语言。系统还支持外部原子（通过Lua调用）、用户交互模式以及B和BC等其他动作语言的扩展翻译。

Conclusion: Cplus2ASP V2是一个高效且功能丰富的动作语言C+推理系统，通过现代答案集求解技术实现了性能飞跃，同时保持了兼容性和可扩展性。

Abstract: We present Version 2 of system Cplus2ASP, which implements the definite fragment of action language C+. Its input language is fully compatible with the language of the Causal Calculator Version 2, but the new system is significantly faster thanks to modern answer set solving techniques. The translation implemented in the system is a composition of several recent theoretical results. The system orchestrates a tool chain, consisting of f2lp, clingo, iclingo, and as2transition. Under the incremental execution mode, the system translates a C+ description into the input language of iclingo, exploiting its incremental grounding mechanism. The correctness of this execution is justified by the module theorem extended to programs with nested expressions. In addition, the input language of the system has many useful features, such as external atoms by means of Lua calls and the user interactive mode. The system supports extensible multi-modal translations for other action languages, such as B and BC, as well.

</details>


### [122] [LLM-Guided Monte Carlo Tree Search over Knowledge Graphs: Composing Mechanistic Explanations for Drug-Disease Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09542)
*Rishabh Jakhar,Michel Dumontier,Remzi Celebi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TESSERA是一个三部分神经符号框架，利用LLM进行局部判别判断，结合知识图谱的硬结构约束和MCTS的长程搜索与信用分配，实现从知识图谱中提取多步解释。在药物机制阐明任务上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从知识图谱中提取多步解释面临组合爆炸（随深度增加候选激增）和信用分配（路径质量需在长序列中体现）两大挑战。前沿LLM虽强但缺乏保障，且长链组合性能下降。

Method: TESSERA三部分框架：1）LLM担任局部判别判断（非自主多步生成），作为先验策略引导探索和状态评估器提供奖励信号；2）知识图谱定义假设空间，强制执行硬结构约束；3）MCTS协调长程搜索，通过反向传播进行原则性信用分配。

Result: 在两个互补知识图谱的药物机制阐明任务上，TESSERA能忠实于已有生物学知识，同时呈现连贯的替代机制。消融实验证实两个LLM组件的判别贡献均有效。

Conclusion: TESSERA提供了一种在结构化知识上进行组合推理的通用范式，超越了当前药物机制应用场景。

Abstract: Extracting multi-step explanations from knowledge graphs poses a combinatorial challenge requiring both heuristic guidance (as candidates proliferate with depth) and credit assignment (as path quality emerges over extended sequences). Frontier LLMs, strong on knowledge/reasoning benchmarks, offer a compelling source of such heuristics, yet their knowledge comes sans guarantees and compositional performance degrades as chains lengthen. We thus present TESSERA, a 3-part neuro-symbolic framework that uses LLMs in a circumscribed role: for local discriminative judgement rather than autonomous multi-step generation; the knowledge graph then defines the hypothesis space enforcing hard structural constraints, and MCTS coordinates the long-horizon search with principled credit assignment via backpropagation. LLMs perform dual roles as a prior policy biasing exploration and a comparative state evaluator supplying reward signals. Evaluation on drug mechanism elucidation across two complementary knowledge graphs demonstrates fidelity to curated biology while surfacing coherent alternative mechanisms, with ablations confirming discriminative contribution from both LLM components. Beyond its current application, our framework offers a general paradigm for compositional reasoning over structured knowledge.

</details>


### [123] [TIDE-Bench: Task-Aware and Diagnostic Evaluation of Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09544)
*Yize Li,Junzhi Li,Jason Song,Chuxiong Sun,Rui Wang,Changwen Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TIDE-Bench，一个高质量、统一、高效的基准测试，用于评估大语言模型的工具集成推理能力，涵盖多样化任务、全面评估协议和高区分度数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前工具集成推理领域缺乏高质量、统一的评估基准，现有评估在数据集质量、任务多样性、诊断全面性和评估效率方面存在局限。

Method: TIDE-Bench通过三个关键优势实现：1) 多样化任务设置（数学推理、知识密集型QA、工具接地实验设计、动态交互任务）；2) 全面且任务感知的评估协议（答案质量、过程可靠性、工具使用效率、推理成本）；3) 通过过滤低区分度实例构建高质量评估集，降低评估成本。

Result: 在多个基础模型和TIR方法上的大量实验揭示了工具接地方面的持续瓶颈，为未来TIR研究提供了洞察。

Conclusion: TIDE-Bench为TIR领域提供了一个高效且全面的评估基准，有助于推动工具集成推理研究的进一步发展。

Abstract: Tool-integrated reasoning has emerged as a promising paradigm for enhancing large language models with external computation, retrieval, and execution capabilities. However, the field still lacks a high-quality and unified evaluation benchmark, and existing TIR evaluations remain limited in dataset quality, task diversity, diagnostic comprehensiveness, and evaluation efficiency. In this work, we introduce TIDE-Bench, a holistic and efficient benchmark for evaluating TIR methods, featuring three key advantages. First, it provides diverse task settings, combining widely used mathematical reasoning and knowledge-intensive QA tasks with two newly designed tasks, namely the tool-grounded experimental design task and the dynamic interactive task, to probe models' abilities in complex tool invocation and multi-tool coordination. Second, TIDE-Bench adopts a comprehensive yet task-aware evaluation protocol, jointly measuring final answer quality, process reliability, tool-use efficiency, and inference cost across heterogeneous task settings. Third, TIDE-Bench constructs high-quality and discriminative evaluation sets by filtering low-discrimination instances from existing datasets, substantially reducing evaluation cost while focusing on more challenging samples. Extensive experiments on multiple foundation models and TIR methods reveal persistent bottlenecks in tool grounding, offering insights for future TIR research.

</details>


### [124] [PDEAgent-Bench: A Multi-Metric, Multi-Library Benchmark for PDE Solver Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09636)
*Zhen Hang,Yushan Yashengjiang,Junhui Li,Huanshuo Dong,Yang Wei,Zhezheng Hao,Jiangtao Ma,Songlin Bai,Haozhong Kai,Xihang Yue,Gangzong Si,Dongming Jiang,Chao Yao,Zhanhua Hu,Jiangqing Zhang,Pengwei Liu,Yaomin Shen,Xingyu Ren,Lei Liu,Zikang Xu,Han Li,Qingsong Yao,Hande Dong,Hong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PDEAgent-Bench，这是首个多指标、多库的PDE到求解器代码生成基准测试，包含645个实例，覆盖6个数学类别和11个PDE族，支持DOLFINx、Firedrake和deal.II三个有限元库。实验表明现有LLM和代码代理生成可运行代码的能力尚可，但在准确性和效率要求下通过率大幅下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成基准主要评估语法正确性或预定义测试用例的通过率，缺乏专门针对PDE到求解器代码生成的公开基准。通用代码基准无法充分捕捉数值PDE求解的独特挑战，如确保求解器精度、效率以及与专业FEM库的兼容性。

Method: 构建包含645个实例的多类别、多库基准，采用分阶段评估框架，依次检查生成求解器的可执行性、数值精度和计算效率，每个实例提供面向代理的问题规范、参考解及特定案例的精度和运行时间目标。

Result: 代表性LLM和代码代理实验显示，模型通常能生成可运行代码，但一旦强制执行精度和效率要求，通过率大幅下降。

Conclusion: 当前代码代理在生成数值可靠且高效的PDE求解器方面仍存在明显局限，PDEAgent-Bench提供了一个基于数值PDE求解实际需求的可复现测试平台。

Abstract: PDE-to-solver code generation aims to automatically synthesize executable numerical solvers from partial differential equation (PDE) specifications. This task requires not only understanding the mathematical structure of PDEs, but also selecting appropriate discretization schemes and solver configurations, and correctly implementing the resulting formulations in finite-element method (FEM) libraries. Existing code generation benchmarks mainly evaluate syntactic correctness, or success on predefined test cases. To our knowledge, there is currently no publicly available benchmark specifically for PDE-to-solver code generation, and general-purpose code benchmarks do not fully capture the unique challenges of numerical PDE solution, such as ensuring solver accuracy, efficiency, and compatibility with professional FEM libraries. We introduce PDEAgent-Bench, to the best of our knowledge, the first multi-metric, multi-library benchmark for PDE-to-solver code generation. PDEAgent-Bench contains 645 instances across 6 mathematical categories and 11 PDE families, with common FEM libraries for DOLFINx, Firedrake, and deal.II. Each instance provides an agent-facing problem specification, a reference solution on a prescribed evaluation grid, and case-specific accuracy and runtime targets. PDEAgent-Bench adopts a staged evaluation framework in which generated solvers must sequentially pass executability, numerical accuracy, and computational efficiency checks. Experiments with representative LLMs and code agents show that models can often produce runnable code, but their pass rate drops substantially once accuracy and efficiency requirements are enforced. These results indicate that current agents remain limited in producing numerically reliable and efficient PDE solvers, and that PDEAgent-Bench provides a reproducible testbed grounded in the practical requirements of numerical PDE solving.

</details>


### [125] [Workspace Optimization: How to Train Your Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09650)
*Elad Sarafian,Gal Kaplun,Ron Banner,Daniel Soudry,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"工作空间优化"概念，通过演化智能体的外部结构化基板（workspace）来提升性能，类似权重空间训练。DreamTeam多智能体框架在ARC-AGI-3上将SOTA从36%提升至38.4%，同时减少31%的环境动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型构建的智能体无法调整其权重，需要探索除权重外还可训练的部分。智能体通过与环境的交互学习，需要一个可演化的外部工作空间。

Method: 提出"工作空间优化"，将人工制品映射为参数、证据映射为数据、反例映射为损失、文本反馈映射为梯度。实现为DreamTeam多智能体框架，包含构建世界模型、规划、假设、探测、策略和路由失败等角色。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-3公共集的25个游戏上，DreamTeam将SOTA协议匹配智能体的得分从36%提升至38.4%，同时每个游戏使用的环境动作减少31%。

Conclusion: 工作空间优化是一种有前景的方向，通过演化外部结构化基板而非调整模型权重，可以有效提升智能体在复杂多轮交互环境中的性能。

Abstract: Modern agents built on frontier language models often cannot adapt their weights. What, then, remains trainable? We argue it is the agent's \emph{workspace}, the structured external substrate it reads, writes, and tests; we call its evolution workspace optimization. Workspace optimization targets hard multi-turn environments where a frontier model has strong priors but cannot solve the task in a single shot, so the agent must learn through interaction. We propose a principled way to evolve the workspace, mirroring the structure of weight-space training: artifacts in place of parameters, evidence in place of data, counterexamples in place of losses, and textual feedback in place of gradients. We instantiate the idea in DreamTeam, a multi-agent harness for ARC-AGI-3 whose roles build an executable world model, plan, hypothesize, probe, strategize, and route failures. On the current 25-game ARC-AGI-3 public set under the official scoring protocol and averaged over two independent runs, DreamTeam improves the SOTA protocol-matched agent's score from 36% to 38.4%, while using 31% fewer environment actions per game.

</details>


### [126] [CodeClinic: Evaluating Automation of Coding Skills for Clinical Reasoning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09675)
*Timothy Ossowski,Xinchi Liu,Danyal Maqbool,Vaibhav Dhanuka,Sheng Zhang,Hoifung Poon,Majid Afshar,Tyler Bradshaw,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了CodeClinic，一个基于MIMIC-IV的基准测试，用于评估LLM智能体能否自主合成和组合可复用的临床技能，而非依赖固定工具库。同时提出了一种离线自动形式化流水线，将自然语言临床指南转化为可重用、可验证的Python技能库，相比零样本代码生成，在提升一致性的同时将每次查询的token使用量减少40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床推理智能体依赖专家手动维护的临床工具库（如脓毒症检测、器官衰竭评估），维护成本高；而零样本查询或代码生成在特定机构临床政策下会产生低效且不可靠的推理链。因此需要探索让LLM智能体自主合成和组合可复用临床技能的方法。

Method: 1) 构建CodeClinic基准，包含两个互补任务：纵向ICU监护（每4小时结构化决策，覆盖25个发现和8个临床家族）和组合性信息检索（63k实例、259个任务、9个领域，按组合依赖深度分层）；2) 提出离线自动形式化流水线，通过迭代LLM优化将自然语言临床指南转化为可重用且经过验证的Python技能库。

Result: 与零样本代码生成相比，自动形式化生成的技能库在提升推理一致性的同时，将每次查询的token使用量减少了40%。

Conclusion: CodeClinic为评估LLM智能体在临床推理中自主合成可复用技能提供了标准化基准，自动形式化方法有效降低了推理成本并提升了可靠性，为临床AI从固定工具库向动态技能合成的范式转变提供了支持。

Abstract: Clinical reasoning agents based on large language models (LLMs) aim to automate tasks such as intensive care unit (ICU) monitoring and patient state tracking from electronic health records (EHRs). Existing systems typically rely on manually curated clinical tools or skills for concepts such as sepsis detection and organ failure assessment. However, maintaining these tool libraries requires substantial expert effort, while zero-shot querying or code generation often produces inefficient and unreliable reasoning chains, especially under institution-specific clinical policies. We introduce CodeClinic, a benchmark built on MIMIC-IV for evaluating whether LLM agents can synthesize and compose reusable clinical skills instead of relying on fixed toolboxes. The benchmark contains two complementary tasks: longitudinal ICU surveillance and compositional information seeking. The longitudinal setting simulates monitoring patient trajectories with structured decisions every four hours across 25 findings and eight clinical families, while the compositional setting spans 63k instances across 259 tasks in nine domains and is stratified by compositional dependency depth to evaluate increasingly complex multi-step reasoning. We further propose an offline autoformalization pipeline that converts natural-language clinical guidelines into reusable and verified Python skill libraries through iterative LLM refinement. Compared with zero-shot code generation, the resulting libraries improve consistency while reducing per-query token usage by up to 40%.

</details>


### [127] [Absurd World: A Simple Yet Powerful Method to Absurdify the Real-world for Probing LLM Reasoning Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09678)
*Ryan Albright,Golam Md Muktadir,Zarif Ikram,S M Jubaer,Mehrab Hossain,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Absurd World 基准测试框架，通过将现实世界模型分解为符号、动作、序列和事件并自动生成逻辑一致但脱离现实的场景，来测试 LLM 是否真正具备逻辑推理能力，而非依赖现实世界的模式记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究聚焦于用越来越复杂的问题来打破 LLM 的推理，但 LLM 在简单逻辑推理中的鲁棒性尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 将现实世界模型分解为符号、动作、序列和事件，自动修改这些要素生成逻辑一致但荒谬的世界（即 Absurd World），保持解决任务所需的逻辑不变。

Result: 在大量模型上使用简单和高级提示技术进行评估，证明 Absurd World 能有效判断 LLM 忽略现实世界模式、进行纯逻辑推理的能力。

Conclusion: 该框架可用于广泛测试 LLM 在现实问题上的推理鲁棒性，验证其是否真正具备逻辑推理能力。

Abstract: While extremely powerful and versatile at various tasks, the thinking capabilities of large language models (LLMs) are often put under scrutiny as they sometimes fail to solve problems that humans can systematically solve. However, recent literature focuses on breaking LLM reasoning with increasingly complex problems, and whether an LLM is robust in simple logical reasoning remains underexplored. This paper proposes Absurd World, a benchmarking framework, to test LLMs against altered realism, where scenarios are logically coherent, and humans can easily solve the tasks. Absurd World breaks a real-world model into symbols, actions, sequences, and events, which are automatically altered to create absurd worlds where the logic to solve the tasks remains the same. It evaluates a large collection of models with simple and advanced prompting techniques, and proves that it is an effective tool to determine LLMs' ability to think logically, ignoring the patterns learned from the real world. One can use this framework to extensively test an LLM against a real-world problem to verify whether the LLM's reasoning capability is robust against variations of the task.

</details>


### [128] [Unpredictability dissociates from structured control in language agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09692)
*Jia Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过大规模实验证明，随机不可预测性无法替代语言智能体中的结构化控制机制（理由、记忆、自我状态和抑制）。在74,352次基准测试和26,946次匹配界面控制中，结构化控制始终优于高随机性变体；57,816次评分记录进一步证实结构化控制在所有数据集上全面领先。后期扩展到不同模型规模和框架仍得到一致结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验随机采样是否能替代语言智能体中耦合理由、记忆、自我状态和抑制的结构化动作选择机制，澄清"不可预测行为即控制"这一常见误解。

Method: 在语言智能体实现中选择性禁用控制组件，进行七数据集基准病灶矩阵（74,352次调用）、匹配界面控制（26,946次生成）、无自由形式措辞的原始行为测试（57,816条记录），以及扩展到Qwen2.5和Mistral-7B系列的多模型、多框架验证，辅以三标注者盲审。

Result: 高随机性比较器在7/7数据集中比结构化控制变体更不可预测；靶向理由和否决病灶在7/7数据集中降低了结构化控制预期特征。结构化智能体在所有数据集上优于所有对照。扩展到更大模型和独立模型族后，各种控制均无法恢复结构化动作控制。三标注者盲审保持高度一致。

Conclusion: 在该实现的智能体家族中，随机不可预测性无法复现结构化的、与动作耦合的控制行为，表明随机采样不能替代结构化控制机制。

Abstract: Unpredictable behavior is often taken as evidence of control, yet stochastic dispersion and structured action control need not coincide. This paper tests whether stochastic sampling can substitute for structured mechanisms that couple reasons, memory, self-state and inhibition to action selection in a language-agent implementation whose control components can be selectively disabled. In a seven-dataset baseline lesion matrix comprising 74,352 calls, the high-stochasticity comparator was more unpredictable than the structured-control variant in 7/7 datasets, whereas targeted reason and veto lesions reduced the expected structured-control profiles in 7/7 datasets each. In a matched-interface control spanning 26,946 generations, the structured agent maintained stronger action-field coupling than all stochastic, post-hoc, scrambled and verbosity controls across every dataset. The primary behavioral test removed free-form trace wording from the evaluation: 57,816 scored records showed the structured-control variant exceeding the high-stochasticity comparator or the reason/veto lesions in 7/7 datasets for all predefined behavioral components. Later open-weight runs extended the no-context controls to Qwen2.5 7B, 14B and 32B and to an independent Mistral-7B family across 20 task families and three agent scaffolds; no-fields, scrambled-context and distribution-matched controls failed to recover structured action control. A three-annotator blinded audit over 1,200 overlap items preserved high agreement. Strict entropy matching, strict token/compute matching and a formal counterfactual-flip stress test did not meet their gates and are treated as limitations. Stochastic unpredictability did not reproduce structured, action-coupled control in this implemented agent family.

</details>


### [129] [Ambig-DS: A Benchmark for Task-Framing Ambiguity in Data-Science Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09698)
*Josefa Lia Stoisser,Marc Boubnovski Martell,Sidsel Boldsen,Kaspar Märtens,Robert Kitchen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Ambig-DS基准套件，用于评估数据科学智能体在任务模糊性下的沉默错误框架问题。通过对预测目标和评估目标两种模糊性的诊断，发现智能体会默默执行错误解读而非崩溃报错，且能否主动提问至关重要，但智能体无法可靠判断何时该提问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只评判数据科学智能体的流水线是否能跑通，忽略了任务定义不明确时智能体是否意识到问题并正确框架任务。随着智能体从协驾向自动驾驶转变，这种"沉默错误框架"成为关键失败模式，亟需系统评估。

Method: 构建两个诊断套件：Ambig-DS-Target（基于DSBench，51个任务，测试预测目标模糊性）和Ambig-DS-Objective（基于MLE-bench，61个任务，测试评估目标模糊性）。每个任务配对明确版与模糊版，经人机验证确保存在多种合理解读。测试五种智能体，分析沉默失败模式，并引入提问机制观察恢复效果。

Result: (i) 失败是沉默的——任务被错误理解但仍产出"好看"结果，而非执行错误；(ii) 允许主动提问能大幅恢复性能，说明任务模糊是性能下降的主要原因；(iii) 智能体无法可靠判断何时提问——宽松提示导致过度提问，保守提示导致对模糊任务默默默认。

Conclusion: 识别目标和评估指标的模糊性（而非流水线执行）才是数据科学智能体评估中缺失的关键瓶颈。现有评估标准需要加入对任务框架能力的检验。

Abstract: As data-science agents shift from co-pilots to auto-pilots, silent misframing becomes a critical failure mode. Agents quietly commit to plausible but unintended task framings, producing clean, executable artifacts that hide their incorrect assessment of the task. Existing benchmarks score whether the pipeline runs, ignoring whether the agent recognized the task was underspecified. We introduce Ambig-DS, two diagnostic suites: one for prediction-target ambiguity (Ambig-DS-Target, 51 tasks built on DSBench, a tabular modeling benchmark) and one for evaluation-objective ambiguity (Ambig-DS-Objective, 61 tasks built on MLE-bench, a Kaggle-style ML competition benchmark), constructed so that scoring uses each source benchmark's original evaluator. For every task we pair the original, fully specified version with an ambiguous variant produced by controlled edits; a human-and-LLM verification pipeline confirms each variant admits multiple plausible interpretations with decision-relevant consequences. The suites are analyzed independently and ambiguity lowers performance in both. Across five agents spanning efficient to frontier-class models, we find in our controlled diagnostic setting: (i) failures are silent commitments: wrong-target submissions on Target, wrong-metric or non-committal baseline submissions on Objective, rather than execution errors; (ii) allowing the agent to ask one clarifying question recovers much of the loss under idealized conditions, suggesting missing framing information drives a substantial part of the observed degradation; but (iii) agents cannot reliably tell when to use it: permissive prompts induce over-asking on clear tasks, while conservative prompts induce silent defaulting on ambiguous ones. Recognizing target and objective underspecification, not pipeline execution, is the bottleneck missing from standard DS-agent evaluations.

</details>


### [130] [Medical Model Synthesis Architectures: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09716)
*Katherine M. Collins,Marlene Berke,Ilia Sucholutsky,Ayman Ali,Adrian Weller,Timothy J. O'Donnell,Tyler Brooke-Wilson,Lionel Wong,Joshua B. Tenenbaum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MedMSA框架，结合语言模型检索与形式化概率模型，实现可校准、可验证的临床不确定性推理，用于鉴别诊断等场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统在临床不确定性推理中缺乏校准能力且推理过程不透明，亟需一种既实用又形式化透明的框架。

Method: MedMSA框架使用语言模型检索相关先验知识，但构建形式化概率模型来支持校准和可验证的不确定性推理。

Result: 在鉴别诊断任务中，能生成包含不确定性权重的潜在诊断列表。

Conclusion: 该框架有望推动安全临床协作，未来可拓展至更广泛的临床应用场景。

Abstract: Medicine is rife with high-stakes uncertainty. Doctors routinely make clinical judgments and decisions that juggle many fundamental unknowns, like predictions about what might be causing a patients' symptoms or decisions about what treatment to try next. Despite increasing interest in developing AI systems that aid or even replace doctors in clinical settings, current systems struggle with calibrated reasoning under uncertainty, and are often deeply opaque about their reasoning. We propose a framework for AI systems that can make practically useful but formally transparent clinical predictions under uncertainty. Given a clinical situation, our framework (MedMSA) uses language models to retrieve relevant prior knowledge, but constructs a formal probabilistic model to support calibrated and verifiable inferences under uncertainty. We show how an initial proof-of-concept of this framework can be used for differential diagnosis, producing an uncertainty-weighted list of potential diagnoses that could explain a patients' symptoms, and discuss future applications and directions for applying this framework more generally for safe clinical collaborations.

</details>


### [131] [Primal-Dual Guided Decoding for Constrained Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09749)
*Federico Tomasi,Dmitrii Moor,Alice Wang,Mounia Lalmas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种 primal-dual guided decoding 方法，通过在离散扩散模型的推理阶段添加约束感知的 logit 偏置，实现对全局属性约束的实时满足，无需重训练，并在文本、分子和音乐三个领域验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型在生成结构化序列时难以强制执行全局属性约束，现有方法缺乏推理时的高效约束控制。

Method: 将约束生成问题形式化为 KL 正则化优化问题，通过在线自适应拉格朗日乘子（mirror descent 更新）在每个去噪步骤修改 token logits，加入约束依赖的偏置项，该偏置是最优 KL 正则化投影的结果。

Result: 在主题文本生成、分子设计和音乐播放列表生成三个任务上，该方法在提高约束满足度的同时，保持了领域相关的质量指标。

Conclusion: 提出了一种无需重训练、无需额外模型评估的通用推理时约束求解框架，支持多约束并行，并提供约束违反的形式化界。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models generate structured sequences by progressively unmasking tokens, but enforcing global property constraints during generation remains an open challenge. We propose primal-dual guided decoding, an inference-time method that formulates constrained generation as a KL-regularised optimisation problem and solves it online via adaptive Lagrangian multipliers. At each denoising step, the method modifies token logits through an additive, constraint-dependent bias, with multipliers updated by mirror descent based on constraint violation. The bias arises as the optimal KL-regularised projection of the constraint, so the constrained distribution remains as close as possible to the model's unconstrained distribution while still satisfying the constraint. The method requires no retraining and no additional model evaluations beyond standard sampling, supports multiple simultaneous constraints, and provides formal bounds on constraint violation. We evaluate our approach on topical text generation, molecular design, and music playlist generation, showing that a single algorithm instantiated via domain-specific scoring functions improves constraint satisfaction while preserving relevant domain-specific quality metrics.

</details>


### [132] [UTS at PsyDefDetect: Multi-Agent Councils and Absence-Based Reasoning for Defense Mechanism Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09769)
*Dima Galat,Marian-Andrei Rizoiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于多阶段协商的Gemini 2.5智能体系统，用于对情感支持对话中的心理防御机制进行分类，在64支队伍中获得第二名（F1=0.406）。核心创新在于将防御机制定义为"缺失"（缺失情感、被阻断的认知、被否认的现实），并将其编码为情感-认知整合光谱的提示级别临床规则（带来+11.4pp F1提升）。架构采用类别特定倡导者评估证据强度的协商机制，无需微调即达F1=0.382（前五）。但发现协商机制对少数类别存在系统性错误，59-80%的稳定少数类预测是错误的，表现为"L7吸引子"现象。最终通过三个微调Qwen3.5模型组成的定向覆盖集成（+2.4pp F1），由结构化多智能体系统（构建者、批评者、回归守卫）选择16个覆盖策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理防御机制分类在情感支持对话中具有重要应用价值，但现有方法难以捕捉防御机制的本质特征——它们由"缺失"（缺失情感、被阻断的认知、被否认的现实）定义。这种负向定义特性使得传统分类方法难以有效处理。

Method: 提出多阶段协商架构：1）构建情感-认知整合光谱的临床规则作为prompt；2）使用Gemini 2.5智能体组成类别特定倡导者，通过评估证据强度而非投票进行协商；3）针对"L7吸引子"问题，设计由三个微调Qwen3.5模型组成的定向覆盖集成，通过结构化多智能体系统（构建者、批评者、回归守卫）选择16个覆盖策略。

Result: 总体F1=0.406，排64支队伍第二。临床规则带来+11.4pp最大单次增益；无微调协商机制达F1=0.382（前五）；定向覆盖集成带来+2.4pp。发现协商机制对少数类存在系统性错误（59-80%稳定预测错误），表现为"L7吸引子"现象。

Conclusion: 基于"缺失"定义的防御机制分类方法是有效的，但需要处理少数类偏差问题。结构化多智能体系统在一次迭代中产生的F1增益超过此前8次尝试的总和，展示了智能体协作的潜力。

Abstract: This paper describes our system for classifying psychological defense mechanisms in emotional support dialogues using the Defense Mechanism Rating Scales (DMRS), placing second (F1 0.406) among 64 teams.1 A central insight is that defense mechanisms are defined by what is absent: missing affect, blocked cognition, denied reality. We encode this as an affect-cognition integration spectrum in prompt-level clinical rules, which account for the largest single gain (+11.4pp F1). Our architecture is a multi-phase deliberative council of Gemini 2.5 agents where class-specific advocates rate evidence strength rather than voting, achieving F1 0.382 with no fine-tuning - a top-5 result on its own. We find, however, that the council is confidently wrong about minority classes: 59-80% of stable minority predictions are incorrect, driven by a systematic "L7 attractor" in which emotional content defaults to the majority class. A targeted override ensemble from three fine-tuned Qwen3.5 models applies 16 overrides (+2.4pp), selected by a structured multi-agent system (builder, critic, regression guard) that produced a larger F1 gain in one iteration than 8 prior attempts combined.

</details>


### [133] [Marrying Generative Model of Healthcare Events with Digital Twin of Social Determinants of Health for Disease Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09771)
*Ziquan Wei,Tingting Dan,Guorong Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个结合ICD编码的社会健康决定因素(SDoH)代理变量的生成模型，用于多器官传感器数据与医疗事件的疾病推理，在UKB数据集上表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有疾病预测生成模型主要依赖医院和登记数据的事件级表征，缺乏对社会健康决定因素(SDoH)的显式建模，限制了个性化疾病建模和临床决策支持能力。

Method: 提出条件潜在扩散框架，包含几何扩散模型（用于脑网络图结构时间演化）和表格数据扩散模型（用于其他器官系统），并整合数字化SDoH代理变量进行模拟干预和疾病轨迹推理。

Result: 在UKB数据集上，涵盖脑(44,834)、心脏(23,987)、肝脏(28,722)和肾脏(32,155)的器官特异性影像特征及近50万医疗历史序列，该方法显著优于现有疾病自回归模型和影像特征生成基线。

Conclusion: 融合SDoH代理变量的多器官生成模型能更有效地进行疾病推理和模拟干预，为个性化医疗提供支持。

Abstract: Despite the central role of sensor-derived measurements such as imaging traits and plasma biomarkers in biomedical research and clinical practice, existing generative models for disease prediction largely depend on event-level representations from hospital and registry data. Given the multi-factorial nature of human disease, the absence of explicit modeling of social determinants of health (SDoH), even in the limited form of ICD-coded proxies (chapters Z and V--Y in ICD-10), limits the capacity for personalized disease modeling and clinical decision support. To address this limitation, we propose a generative model with ICD-coded proxies of SDoH for \textit{in silico} modeling of disease reasoning, a conditioned latent diffusion framework that establishes the connection between multi-organ sensor data with tokenized healthcare events. Specifically, we introduce a novel geometric diffusion model to characterize the temporal evolution of complex data representation such as brain networks (region-to-region connectivity encoded in a graph), in parallel with diffusion models for tabular data from other organ systems. Together, we integrate the generative model with digitalized SDoH proxies (coined \modelname{}) for simulated intervention and reasoning of future disease trajectories. We conduct extensive experiments on the UK Biobank (UKB) dataset, which contains organ-specific imaging traits, including brain (44,834), heart (23,987), liver (28,722), and kidney (32,155), along with nearly 500k medical history sequences (age range: 25$\sim$89 years). Our \modelname{} achieves significant improvements over state-of-the-art human disease autoregressive models and imaging trait generative baselines.

</details>


### [134] [Attribution-based Explanations for Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09780)
*Paul Kobialka,Andrea Pferscher,Francesco Leofante,Erika Ábrahám,Silvia Lizeth Tapia Tarifa,Einar Broch Johnsen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的归因技术，用于解释马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中序贯决策模型的决策逻辑，能够为状态和执行路径分配重要性分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的归因技术主要针对静态输入特征，无法推广到序贯决策场景。本文旨在填补这一空白，为MDP中的决策过程提供可解释的归因解释。

Method: 形式化定义了MDP中归因应表示的内容（对状态和路径的重要性分数），并利用策略合成技术来高效计算这些分数，从而处理MDP固有的非确定性。

Result: 在五个案例研究上进行了评估，展示了该方法在提供序贯决策智能体逻辑的可解释洞察方面的实用性。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将归因方法从静态场景扩展到了序贯决策场景，为理解MDP中智能体的决策行为提供了新的可解释性工具。

Abstract: Attribution techniques explain the outcome of an AI model by assigning a numerical score to its inputs. So far, these techniques have mainly focused on attributing importance to static input features at a single point in time, and thus fail to generalize to sequential decision-making settings. This paper fills this gap by introducing techniques to generate attribution-based explanations for Markov Decision Processes (MDPs). We give a formal characterization of what attributions should represent in MDPs, focusing on explanations that assign importance scores to both individual states and execution paths. We show how importance scores can be computed by leveraging techniques for strategy synthesis, enabling the efficient computation of these scores despite the non-determinism inherent in an MDP. We evaluate our approach on five case-studies, demonstrating its utility in providing interpretable insights into the logic of sequential decision-making agents.

</details>


### [135] [EnactToM: An Evolving Benchmark for Functional Theory of Mind in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09826)
*Gurusha Juneja,Dylan Lu,Saaket Agashe,Parth Diwane,Edward Gunn,Jayanth Srinivasa,Gaowen Liu,William Yang Wang,Yali Du,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EnactToM，一个包含300个具身多智能体任务的演进式基准，用于测试功能性心理理论（functional ToM）。在硬分集中，所有七个前沿模型在功能性任务完成上得分为0.0% Pass^3，平均仅45.0%通过字面信念探针，93%的失败源于 epistemic 协调崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ToM基准多通过直接询问信念来测试字面ToM，但缺乏对智能体在具身环境中基于隐式信念做出最优行动的能力（即功能性ToM）的评估，这在多智能体协作中至关重要。

Method: 构建了EnactToM基准，包含300个3D家庭场景中的具身多智能体任务，具有部分可观测性、私有信息和受限通信；每个任务经过形式化验证确保可解性和所需的认知深度，并随着模型进步动态生成更难的任務。

Result: 七个前沿模型在硬分集的功能性任务完成上均为0.0% Pass^3，而字面信念探针平均达45.0%；93%的失败案例可归因于epistemic协调问题，如信息隐瞒、忽略伙伴约束和消息分配错误。

Conclusion: 当前AI模型严重缺乏功能性ToM能力，无法在具身多智能体环境中进行有效的隐性信念推理和协作，亟需针对epistemic协调的新型研究方向。

Abstract: Theory of Mind (ToM), the ability to track others epistemic state, makes humans efficient collaborators. AI agents need the same capacity in multi agent settings, yet existing benchmarks mostly test literal ToM by asking direct belief questions. The ability act optimally on implicit beliefs in embodied environments, called functional ToM, remains largely untested. We introduce EnactToM, an evolving benchmark of 300 embodied multi-agent tasks set in a 3D household with partial observability, private information, and constrained communication. Each task is formally verified for solvability and required epistemic depth, and new tasks are generated increase difficulty as models improve. On the hard split, all seven evaluated frontier models score 0.0% Pass^3 on functional task completion, while averaging 45.0% on literal belief probes. Manual analysis traces 93% of sampled failures to epistemic coordination breakdowns such as withheld information, ignored partner constraints, and misallocated messages, providing a concrete target for future work.

</details>


### [136] [Yield Curve Forecasting using Machine Learning and Econometrics: A Comparative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09842)
*Aman Singh,Tokunbo Ogunfunmi,Sanjiv Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文比较了多种时间序列预测方法（包括计量经济学、经典机器学习和深度学习方法）在美国国债收益率曲线上的表现，使用47年日度数据。结果显示ARIMA和朴素计量经济学模型整体表现最优，而机器学习方法中TimeGPT、LGBM和RNN表现较好。研究还探讨了平稳/非平稳数据对深度学习模型的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习在NLP和CV领域取得了巨大成功，但在时间序列预测（尤其是金融领域）的影响仍存争议。国债收益率曲线对债券市场至关重要，而现有研究缺乏对多种现代方法（尤其是深度学习）在收益率曲线预测上的系统比较。

Method: 使用47年日度美国国债收益率曲线数据，比较了三类方法：计量经济学/时间序列方法（ARIMA及其扩展、朴素基准）、经典机器学习（集成方法）和深度学习方法（RNN、多种Transformer架构、TimeGPT）。同时研究了输入数据平稳性对深度学习模型的影响。

Result: ARIMA和朴素计量经济学模型在整体上优于其他模型，仅在一个时间块中例外。在机器学习方法中，TimeGPT、LGBM和RNN表现最佳。深度学习模型并未显著超越传统方法。

Conclusion: 在国债收益率曲线预测任务上，传统计量经济学方法仍然具有竞争力，机器学习/深度学习的优势并不明显。输入数据的平稳性处理对深度学习模型性能有重要影响。

Abstract: While machine learning has revolutionized many fields such as natural language processing (NLP) and computer vision, its impact on time-series forecasting is still widely disputed, especially in the finance domain. This paper compares forecasting performance on U.S. Treasury yield curve data across econometrics/time-series analysis, classical machine learning, and deep learning methods, using daily data over 47 years. The Treasury yield curve is important because it is widely used by every participant in the bond markets, which are larger than equity markets. We examine a variety of methods that have not been tested on yield curve forecasting, especially deep learning algorithms. The algorithms include the Autoregressive Integrated Moving Average (ARIMA) model and its extensions, naive benchmarks, ensemble methods, Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs), and multiple transformers built for forecasting. ARIMA and naive econometric models outperform other models overall, except in one time block. Of the machine learning methods, TimeGPT, LGBM and RNNs perform the best. Furthermore, the paper explores whether stationary or nonstationary data are more appropriate as input to deep learning models.

</details>


### [137] [The Metacognitive Probe: Five Behavioural Calibration Diagnostics for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09844)
*Rafael C. T. Oliveira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了元认知探针（Metacognitive Probe），一种将LLM的置信行为分解为五个行为维度的诊断工具，并在N=8个前沿模型和N=69个人类上进行了评估。关键发现是Gemini 2.5 Flash内部存在47分的分离现象：在任务内校准上表现最佳（88分），但在跨任务难度预测上表现最差（41分）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准（如MMLU、BIG-Bench等）只能评估模型是否产生正确回答，但无法判断模型是否"知道"自己的回答是错误的。复合校准基准的聚合分数会掩盖模型在特定领域的过度自信问题，因此需要一种能够揭示这些隐性缺陷的诊断工具。

Method: 设计了元认知探针，包含五个任务、15个槽位的诊断工具，将LLM的置信行为分解为五个行为维度：置信校准（T1-CC）、认知警觉（T2-EV）、知识边界（T3-KB）、校准范围（T4-CR）和推理链验证（T5-RCV）。在N=8个前沿模型和N=69个人类上进行评估。

Result: Gemini 2.5 Flash表现出显著的47分内部差异：在任务内校准（T1-CC）上达到88分（组内最佳，Spearman rho=+0.551，p=0.005），但在跨任务难度预测（T4-CR）上仅得41分（组内最差，sigma_conf=1.4）。同时，预设的人类发展假设被证伪。

Conclusion: 复合校准基准的聚合分数具有误导性，元认知探针能够揭示模型在特定维度上的隐性缺陷。该工具并非经过验证的跨物种元认知量表，但可用于诊断LLM的置信行为偏差。

Abstract: The Metacognitive Probe is an exploratory five-task, 15-slot diagnostic that decomposes an LLM's confidence behaviour into five behaviourally-distinct dimensions: confidence calibration (T1-CC), epistemic vigilance (T2-EV), knowledge boundary (T3-KB), calibration range (T4-CR), and reasoning-chain validation (T5-RCV). It is evaluated on N=8 frontier models and N=69 humans. The instrument is motivated by Flavell (1979) and Nelson and Narens (1990) but operates on observable confidence-correctness alignment; it is not a validated cross-species metacognition scale, and the pre-specified human developmental hypothesis was falsified.
  Composite benchmarks (MMLU, BIG-Bench, HELM, GPQA) ask whether a model produces a correct response. They are silent on whether the model knows when its response is wrong. A model can score 80 on a composite calibration benchmark and still be wildly overconfident in narrow pockets the aggregate cannot surface. The Metacognitive Probe surfaces those pockets.
  Our headline is a 47-point within-model dissociation in Gemini 2.5 Flash: panel-best within-task calibration (T1-CC = 88; Spearman rho = +0.551, 95% CI [+0.14, +0.80], p = 0.005) and panel-worst cross-task difficulty prediction (T4-CR = 41; sigma_conf = 1.4 across twelve factoids).

</details>


### [138] [Fairness of Explanations in Artificial Intelligence (AI): A Unifying Framework, Axioms, and Future Direction toward Responsible AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09852)
*Gideon Popoola,John Sheppard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文识别了算法公平性与可解释AI交叉领域中的“程序性偏差”盲点，即模型输出可能满足公平性标准但其推理过程存在根本性不公。论文提出了解释公平性的条件不变性框架，并建立了七维分类法、三种生成机制及六步评估流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法公平性研究和可解释AI研究基本相互独立，导致一个被忽视的问题：一个模型的输出可能满足所有标准公平性准则（如统计均等、机会均等），但其内部的推理过程（即解释）可能存在系统性偏差。这种“程序性偏差”尚未被系统研究和定义，因此需要将解释的公平性作为独立的科学对象进行研究。

Method: 论文采用统一的理论与文献综述方法，首次整合该新兴领域。核心方法是提出条件不变性框架：解释应对所有与任务无关的属性保持不变，即对于所有任务相关特征x，有P(E(X) | X_rel=x_rel, A=a) = P(E(X) | X_rel=x_rel, A=b)。同时构建了七维分类法、识别了三种生成机制（表示驱动、解释模型不匹配、可行动性驱动），并提出六步评估工作流。

Result: 论文发现所有现有的解释公平性指标都可以视作条件不变性原则的部分操作化。系统梳理了三种导致解释不公的机制：表示驱动的偏差（数据或特征表示中存在偏见）、解释模型不匹配（解释方法本身引入偏差）、可行动性驱动的偏差（不同群体对解释的可操作能力不同）。提出了实践中的六步评估工作流。

Conclusion: 解释的公平性是一个独立且重要的研究问题，不能仅通过输出层面的公平性保证来覆盖。条件不变性框架为统一理解解释公平性提供了理论基础。后验解释器（post-hoc explainers）在认证解释公平性方面存在固有缺陷。需要建立新的方法论来确保模型的推理过程本身是公平的，而不仅仅是其决策结果。

Abstract: Machine learning algorithms are being used in high-stakes decisions, including those in criminal justice, healthcare, credit, and employment. The research community has responded with two largely independent research fields: \emph{algorithmic fairness}, which targets equitable outcomes, and \emph{explainable AI} (XAI), which targets interpretable reasoning. This survey identifies and maps a novel blind spot at their intersection, which is a model that can satisfy every standard fairness criterion in its outputs while being profoundly unfair in its \emph{reasoning process}. We refer to this as the procedural bias, and mitigating it requires treating the fairness of explanations as a distinct object of scientific study.
  To our knowledge, we provide the first unified theoretical and literature review of this emerging field and elucidate the drawbacks of post-hoc explainers in certifying explanation fairness. Our central contribution is a \emph{conditional invariance framework} formalizing explanation fairness as the requirement that explanations should be indifferent regardless of the protected attributes $ P(E(X) \in \cdot \mid X_\text{rel} = x_\text{rel},\, A = a) = P(E(X) \in \cdot \mid X_\text{rel} = x_\text{rel},\, A = b)$ for all task-relevant $x$, a single principle from which all existing explanation fairness metrics emerge as partial operationalizations. We introduce a seven-dimensional taxonomy, identify three generative mechanisms of explanation inequity (representation-driven, explanation-model mismatch, actionability-driven), and propose a canonical six-step evaluation workflow for operationalizing explanation fairness audits in practice.

</details>


### [139] [When to Re-Commit: Temporal Abstraction Discovery for Long-Horizon Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09860)
*Chen Li,Zhantao Yang,Fangyi Chen,Han Zhang,Anudeepsekhar Bolimera,Marios Savvides*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将"承诺深度"（即重新规划前执行的原语动作数量）作为可学习的、状态条件化的策略变量，而非手工设定的固定值。在滑动拼图和推箱子任务上，自适应策略在所有非退化固定深度基线上实现了帕累托最优，解决率提升高达12.5个百分点，同时每个回合使用的原语动作减少约25%。使用7B骨干网络的方法优于GPT-5.5和Claude Sonnet，而所有开源视觉-语言模型的零样本成功率为0%。理论分析表明，当局部最优深度随状态变化时，状态条件化承诺严格优于任何固定深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程推理系统大多将重新规划之间的执行步数（承诺深度）设为手工设定的固定值，但最优深度会随状态变化而变化。本文旨在将承诺深度作为可学习的、状态条件化的变量，从而实现自适应权衡重规划成本与复合执行误差。

Method: 在视觉-语言模型中联合预测"执行什么动作"和"执行多长步数"，将承诺深度作为策略本身的可学习状态条件变量，实现自适应调整。

Result: 在滑动拼图和推箱子任务上，自适应策略在解决率上提升高达12.5个百分点，每个回合使用原语动作减少约25%。7B骨干模型优于GPT-5.5和Claude Sonnet，所有开源VLM零样本成功率为0%。

Conclusion: 状态条件化的承诺深度策略在理论上和实验上都优于任何固定深度策略，能够在减少规划成本的同时提高任务成功率。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning requires deciding not only what actions to take, but how deeply to commit before the next observation. We formalize this as \emph{commitment depth}: the number of primitive actions executed open-loop between replans. Commitment depth induces a trade-off between replanning cost and compounding execution error, yet most existing long-horizon systems fix it as a hand-designed scalar. In this work, we instead treat commitment depth as a learnable, state-conditioned variable of the policy itself. We instantiate this within a model-native vision--language policy that jointly predicts both what to execute and for how long. Across Sliding Puzzle and Sokoban, the resulting adaptive policy Pareto-dominates every non-degenerate fixed-depth baseline, achieving up to 12.5 percentage points higher solve rate while using approximately 25\% fewer primitive actions per episode. Despite using a 7B backbone, our method outperforms GPT-5.5 and Claude Sonnet on both tasks, while every tested open-weight vision--language model achieves 0\% zero-shot success. We further present a theoretical analysis showing that, under the standard commitment-depth surrogate, state-conditioned commitment strictly dominates any fixed depth whenever the locally optimal depth varies across states.

</details>


### [140] [Cross-Family Universality of Behavioral Axes via Anchor-Projected Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09875)
*Su-Hyeon Kim,Yo-Sub Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出锚点投影框架（ACS），将不同LLM家族的隐藏表示映射到共享坐标空间，实现跨家族行为方向的可迁移性，无需微调即可在目标模型上重构规范方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同LLM家族使用不同的隐藏维度、分词器和训练流程，导致行为方向难以跨模型比较或迁移，亟需一个统一的表示空间来实现可解释性的跨家族泛化。

Method: 通过锚点投影框架将源模型的隐藏表示映射到共享的锚点坐标空间（ACS），在ACS中提取并平均行为方向得到规范方向，再通过锚点激活重构到目标模型的原始隐藏空间，无需微调或目标特定方向提取。

Result: 在5个指令调优模型家族和10个行为轴上，Llama-Qwen-Mistral-Phi（LQMP）集群在ACS中同轴方向紧密对齐；LQMP集群在十路检测准确率达0.83，平均二元AUROC达0.95，规范方向可引发拒绝率变化高达+0.46%。

Conclusion: ACS提供了跨家族可解释性的新视角，证明表示级别的迁移在不同模型家族间依然鲁棒，且少量源模型和锚点池即可近似可迁移方向。

Abstract: Large language models from different families use different hidden dimensions, tokenizers, and training procedures, making behavioral directions difficult to compare or transfer across models. We introduce an anchor-projection framework that maps hidden representations from each model into a shared anchor coordinate space (ACS). Behavioral directions extracted from source models are projected into ACS and averaged into a canonical direction. For a new model, the canonical direction is reconstructed into its native hidden space using only anchor activations, without fine-tuning or target-specific direction extraction. We evaluate five instruction-tuned model families and ten behavioral axes. We find that same-axis directions align tightly across the Llama-Qwen-Mistral-Phi (LQMP) cluster in ACS. This shared structure transfers to downstream tasks. For the aligned LQMP cluster, held-out targets achieve (0.83) ten-way detection accuracy and (0.95) mean binary AUROC, while canonical steering induces refusal-rate shifts of up to +0.46% under distribution shift. Sensitivity analyses show that two source models and small anchor pools already suffice to approximate transferable directions. Overall, ACS provides a novel perspective on cross-family interpretability, revealing that representation-level transfer remains robust across model families.

</details>


### [141] [M2A: Synergizing Mathematical and Agentic Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09879)
*Junjian Wang,Xin Zhou,Qiran Xu,Kun Zhan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出M2A范式，通过模型合并（model merging）来协同数学推理与智能体推理，在不重新训练模型的情况下提升智能体推理能力，在SWE-Bench Verified上将Qwen3-8B的解决率从44.0%提升至51.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学推理（封闭世界、单轮回答）与智能体推理（多轮交互、行动与思考交错）之间存在范式错配，导致两者难以相互受益，多任务学习下推理行为不稳定且性能提升有限。

Method: 提出M2A范式，直接在参数空间操作：识别对智能体行为至关重要的特征子空间（特征子空间），将数学推理任务向量仅沿其零空间（null space）进行合并，从而在不扰动智能体行为的前提下注入推理能力。无需额外梯度更新，合并系数可作为控制推理长度的简单旋钮。

Result: 在真实代码智能体场景中，M2A有效扩展了智能体推理深度，将Qwen3-8B在SWE-Bench Verified上的解决率从44.0%提升至51.2%，且无需重新训练模型。

Conclusion: M2A通过参数空间的零空间合并策略，成功解耦了数学推理与智能体推理之间的冲突，为多任务推理能力的高效融合提供了一种轻量级、无需额外训练的可行方案。

Abstract: While reasoning has become a central capability of large language models (LLMs), the reasoning patterns required for different scenarios are often misaligned. Mathematical reasoning typically relies on intrinsic logic to solve closed-world problems in a single response, whereas agentic reasoning requires not only internal reasoning but also multi-turn interaction with external environments, interleaving thought and action. This misalignment prevents mathematical and agentic reasoning from effectively benefiting from each other, often yielding unstable reasoning behavior and only limited performance gains under multi-task learning. In this paper, we propose M2A, a novel paradigm that synergizes mathematical and agentic reasoning via model merging. To avoid overfitting to superficial reasoning patterns under joint training, M2A operates directly in parameter space: it identifies the feature subspace critical for agent behavior, and merges the mathematical reasoning task vector only along its null space, thereby injecting reasoning capability along directions that do not perturb agent behavior. Unlike SFT or RL, M2A requires no additional gradient-update and exposes the merging coefficient as a simple knob for controlling reasoning length. Experiments in a challenging real-world coding agent setting show that our method effectively extends agentic reasoning depth and delivers substantial performance improvements. Applied to a fine-tuned Qwen3-8B, M2A improves its SWE-Bench Verified resolved rate from 44.0% to 51.2% without retraining the model. Code is available at https://github.com/laplucky/M2A.git.

</details>


### [142] [The Gordian Knot for VLMs: Diagrammatic Knot Reasoning as a Hard Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09900)
*Hao Liu,Jicheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KnotBench是一个包含858,318张结图的数据集，用于评估视觉语言模型。测试了Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5在14个任务上的表现，结果多数接近随机水平，模型无法进行结的符号转录和操作推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型能"看到"结图但无法基于其结构采取行动，存在感知与操作之间的鸿沟。作者希望系统性地评测和定位这一缺陷。

Method: 构建KnotBench数据集（858,318张图，1,951个原型结），设计14个任务（4个家族：等价判断、移动预测、识别、跨模态对齐），用Regina规范签名验证答案。测试Claude Opus 4.7和GPT-5（含/不含思维链），64K token输出预算。

Result: 56个（任务, 模型）组合中15个等于或低于随机基线；14个任务中8个最佳得分低于1.5倍随机；所有模型无法生成正确的结图到符号转录；思维链使Claude提升1.65分，GPT-5提升9.25分。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型能抓住结图的部分特征，但缺乏在这些特征上模拟操作的能力，即存在感知-操作鸿沟。

Abstract: A vision-language model can look at a knot diagram and report what it sees, yet fail to act on that structure. KnotBench pairs an 858,318-image corpus from 1,951 prime-knot prototypes (crossing numbers 3 to 19) with a protocol whose answers are checked against Regina's canonical knot signature. Its 14 tasks span four families, equivalence judgment, move prediction, identification, and cross-modal grounding; an image-versus-symbol split locates failures along the perception-operation gap. We score Claude Opus 4.7 and GPT-5, each with and without thinking, under a 64K output-token budget matched on both vendors. Across 56 (task, model) cases, 15 sit at or below a random baseline and 8 of 14 tasks have a best score under 1.5x random. On diagram-to-symbol transcription, no model produces a strictly correct string, and permissive Regina decoding recovers the knot in 0 to 4 of 100 items. Thinking-mode reasoning lifts overall accuracy by 1.65 points for Claude and 9.25 points for GPT-5, narrowing the gap only modestly. Read together, the four families suggest current vision-language models hold features of a diagram but lack apparatus to simulate moves on those features.

</details>


### [143] [Separate First, Fuse Later: Mitigating Cross-Modal Interference in Audio-Visual LLMs Reasoning with Modality-Specific Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09906)
*Xuanchen Li,Yuheng Lu,Chenrui Cui,Tianrui Wang,Zikang Huang,Yu Jiang,Long Zhou,Longbiao Wang,Jianwu Dang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"先分离后融合"(SFFL)框架，通过分模态链式推理和强化学习，减少音视频问答中的跨模态干扰与幻觉，在多项基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频大语言模型存在跨模态干扰问题，即一个模态的信息误导另一模态的解读，导致幻觉产生。作者将此归因于中间推理过程中不受控制的跨模态交互。

Method: 1) SFFL框架：强制进行模态特定的链式推理，分别生成音频和视频推理轨迹，再融合证据回答问题。2) 构建模态偏好标签的数据流水线，作为辅助奖励用于强化学习，鼓励实例级别上对模态线索的自适应偏好。3) 模态特定推理机制：分离推理阶段保持模态隔离，证据融合阶段允许完全访问跨模态信息。

Result: 通用AVQA基准平均提升5.16%，跨模态幻觉基准提升11.17%。

Conclusion: SFFL通过分离推理、后期融合的策略，有效减少了跨模态干扰，在准确性和鲁棒性上均优于基线方法，尤其在高幻觉场景下表现突出。

Abstract: Audio and vision provide complementary evidence for audio-visual question answering, yet current audio-visual large language models may suffer from cross-modal interference: information from one modality misguides the interpretation of another, thereby inducing hallucinations. We attribute this issue to uncontrolled cross-modal interactions during intermediate reasoning. To mitigate this, we propose Separate First, Fuse Later (SFFL), an audio-visual reasoning framework designed to reduce cross-modal interference. SFFL enforces modality-specific chain-of-thought reasoning, producing separate audio and visual reasoning traces and integrating evidence for answering. We construct modality-preference labels via a data pipeline under different modality input settings. We use these labels as an auxiliary reward in reinforcement learning to encourage a instance-dependent preference for modality cues when answering. We further introduce a modality-specific reasoning mechanism that preserves modality isolation during the separated reasoning stage while enabling full access to cross-modal information at the evidence fusion stage. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in both accuracy and robustness, yielding an average relative gain of 5.16\% on general AVQA benchmarks and 11.17\% on a cross-modal hallucination benchmark.

</details>


### [144] [RADAR: Redundancy-Aware Diffusion for Multi-Agent Communication Structure Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09907)
*Zhen Zhang,Wanjing Zhou,Juncheng Li,Hao Fei,Jun Wen,Wei Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RADAR是一个冗余感知且查询自适应的生成框架，通过逐步生成通信拓扑来降低多智能体系统的通信开销，同时提升准确率、鲁棒性和token效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多智能体系统依赖固定或单步生成的通信拓扑，导致简单任务浪费token，复杂任务能力受限，缺乏细粒度结构探索和灵活组合。

Method: 利用条件离散图扩散模型，将通信拓扑设计建模为逐步生成过程，并通过有效图大小（effective size）进行引导，实现冗余感知和查询自适应的通信结构优化。

Result: 在六个基准测试中，RADAR在准确率、token消耗和鲁棒性上均持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: RADAR通过动态生成通信拓扑有效平衡了多智能体系统的效率与能力，验证了图扩散模型在拓扑设计中的潜力。

Abstract: Compared with individual agents, large language model based multi-agent systems have shown great capabilities consistently across diverse tasks, including code generation, mathematical reasoning, and planning, etc. Despite their impressive performance, the effectiveness and robustness of these systems heavily rely on their communication topology, which is often fixed or generated in a single step. This restricts fine-grained structural exploration and flexible composition, resulting in excessive token utilization on simple tasks while limiting capability on complicated tasks. To mitigate this challenge, we introduce RADAR, a redundancy-aware and query-adaptive generative framework that actively reduce communication overhead. Motivated by recent progress in conditional discrete graph diffusion models, we formulate communication topology design as a step-by-step generation process, guided by the effective size of the graph. Comprehensive experiments on six benchmarks demonstrate that RADAR consistently outperforms recent baselines, achieving higher accuracy, lower token consumption, and greater robustness across diverse scenarios. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/cszhangzhen/RADAR.

</details>


### [145] [expo: Exploration-prioritized policy optimization via adaptive kl regulation and gaussian curriculum sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09923)
*Mingxiong Lin,Zhangquan Gong,Maowen Tang,Qian Li,Chuangchuang Wang,Jian Ma,Sutian Huang,Kai Tang,Haonan Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EXPO算法，通过动态KL调节（AKL）和高斯课程采样（GCS）两个轻量模块改进GRPO在数学推理中的效率问题，在多个基准上显著提升性能，尤其在AIME 2025 pass@32上从63.33%提升至76.67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRPO算法存在两个未被充分研究的低效问题：(1) 固定的KL惩罚系数限制了模型在需要显著偏离参考策略时的探索；(2) 均匀采样训练问题忽视了中等难度问题能提供最有信息量的梯度信号。

Method: 提出EXPO框架，包含两个即插即用模块：(1) Accuracy-Conditioned KL Scaling (AKL) — 根据批次平均准确率动态调整KL正则化强度；(2) Gaussian Curriculum Sampling (GCS) — 以中等难度（准确率约0.5）为中心的高斯分布分配问题采样权重。

Result: 在DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B和Qwen3-8B-Base上，EXPO在六个数学推理基准上稳定超越GRPO。AIME 2025 pass@32从63.33%提升至76.67%（绝对增益13.34），8B模型平均pass@32提升2.66。pass@32的提升远大于pass@1。

Conclusion: EXPO通过动态KL调节和课程采样有效扩大了模型在固定推理预算下的探索边界，验证了中等难度问题对策略优化的重要性以及自适应KL惩罚的必要性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has become the standard paradigm for LLM mathematical reasoning, where Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) serves as the mainstream algorithm. We point out two understudied inefficiencies existing in GRPO. First, the fixed KL penalty coefficient overly restricts policy exploration at stages where the model requires significant deviation from the reference policy. Second, uniform sampling of training questions ignores that moderately difficult problems provide the most informative gradient signals for optimization. We propose Exploration-Prioritized Policy Optimization (EXPO) with two lightweight plug-in modules. The Accuracy-Conditioned KL Scaling (AKL) dynamically adjusts KL regularization strength through a smooth nonlinear function of batch average accuracy, relaxing the penalty when the model underperforms and strengthening it when the model achieves good results. The Gaussian Curriculum Sampling (GCS) assigns sampling weights to questions following a Gaussian distribution centered at moderate accuracy around 0.5, focusing training on the model's learning frontier. We conduct extensive experiments on DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-1.5B and Qwen3-8B-Base over six mathematical reasoning benchmarks. The results show EXPO steadily surpasses vanilla GRPO. It obtains an absolute gain of 13.34 on AIME 2025 pass@32, rising from 63.33 percent to 76.67 percent, and achieves an average pass@32 improvement of 2.66 on the 8B model. The much larger performance gains on pass@32 compared with pass@1 demonstrate that EXPO effectively enlarges the model's exploration boundary under a fixed inference cost budget.

</details>


### [146] [HAGE: Harnessing Agentic Memory via RL-Driven Weighted Graph Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09942)
*Dongming Jiang,Yi Li,Guanpeng Li,Qiannan Li,Bingzhe Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HAGE提出了一个加权多关系记忆框架，将检索重构为对统一关系记忆图的序列化、查询条件化遍历，通过可训练的关系特征向量、路由网络和强化学习训练，提升了长程推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体LLM系统中的记忆检索通常被视为静态查找问题，依赖扁平向量搜索或固定二元关系图，无法捕捉事件之间关系的强度、置信度和查询相关性的动态变化。

Method: 构建多关系记忆图，每个边具有可训练的关系特征向量；使用LLM分类器识别查询的关系意图；路由网络动态调制边嵌入的对应维度；通过语义相似度和查询条件化边表示的联合学习计算遍历分数；采用强化学习框架联合优化路由行为和边表示。

Result: 在长程推理准确性上表现更优，相比最先进的智能体记忆系统获得了更好的准确性-效率平衡。

Conclusion: 将记忆检索从静态查找转变为动态、查询条件化的图遍历，能够更灵活地利用关系结构，提升智能体系统的长程推理能力。

Abstract: Memory retrieval in agentic large language model (LLM) systems is often treated as a static lookup problem, relying on flat vector search or fixed binary relational graphs. However, fixed graph structures cannot capture the varying strength, confidence, and query-dependent relevance of relationships between events. In this paper, we propose HAGE, a weighted multi-relational memory framework that reconceptualizes retrieval as sequential, query-conditioned traversal over a unified relational memory graph. Memory is organized as relation-specific graph views over shared memory nodes, where each edge is associated with a trainable relation feature vector encoding multiple relational signals. Given a query, an LLM-based classifier identifies the relational intent, and a routing network dynamically modulates the corresponding dimensions of the edge embedding. Traversal scores are computed via a learned combination of semantic similarity and these query-conditioned edge representations. This allows memory traversal to prioritize high-utility relational paths while softly suppressing noisy or weakly relevant connections. Beyond adaptive traversal, HAGE further introduces a reinforcement learning-based training framework that jointly optimizes routing behavior and edge representations using downstream tasks. Finally, empirical results demonstrate improved long-horizon reasoning accuracy and a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off compared to state-of-the-art agentic memory systems. Our code is available at https://github.com/FredJiang0324/HAGE_MVPReview.

</details>


### [147] [LoopVLA: Learning Sufficiency in Recurrent Refinement for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09948)
*Boyang Shen,Kaixiang Yang,Hao Wang,Qiuyu Yu,Qiang Xie,Qiang Li,Zhiwei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoopVLA提出一种循环VLA架构，通过共享Transformer块进行迭代表示精炼，并引入自监督分布对齐目标来学习动作预测的充分性估计，在减少45%参数的同时提升1.7倍推理吞吐量，且任务成功率匹配或超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将视觉-语言骨干的最深层表示视为动作预测的通用最优表示，但机器人操作需要频繁的闭环空间调整，过度抽象会浪费计算并削弱底层几何线索。早期退出策略仅靠预定义层或启发式规则，无法直接判断表示何时足够用于动作预测。

Method: 提出LoopVLA循环架构：(1) 共享Transformer块迭代精炼多模态token；(2) 每次迭代输出候选动作和充分性分数；(3) 参数跨迭代共享，解耦精炼与绝对层索引；(4) 自监督分布对齐目标训练中间置信度分数匹配相对动作质量，将充分性学习与策略优化信号关联。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus和VLA-Arena上，参数减少45%，推理吞吐量提升最高1.7倍，任务成功率匹配或超越强基线。

Conclusion: LoopVLA通过循环精炼与自监督充分性估计，实现了VLA策略的效率-性能前沿突破，表明表示精炼的轮次比层数更重要，且充分性可从策略信号中自监督学习。

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models typically treat the deepest representation of a vision-language backbone as universally optimal for action prediction. However, robotic manipulation is composed of many frequent closed-loop spatial adjustments, for which excessive abstraction may waste computation and weaken low-level geometric cues essential for precise control. Existing early-exit strategies attempt to reduce computation by stopping at predefined layers or applying heuristic rules such as action consistency, but they do not directly answer when a representation is actually sufficient for action. In this paper, we present LoopVLA, a recurrent VLA architecture that jointly learns representation refinement, action prediction, and sufficiency estimation. LoopVLA iteratively applies a shared Transformer block to refine multimodal tokens, and at each iteration produces both a candidate action and a sufficiency score that estimates whether further refinement is necessary. By sharing parameters across iterations, LoopVLA decouples refinement from absolute layer indices and grounds sufficiency estimation in the evolving representation itself. Since sufficiency has no direct supervision, we introduce a self-supervised distribution alignment objective, where intermediate confidence scores are trained to match the relative action quality across refinement steps, thereby linking sufficiency learning to policy optimization signals. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and VLA-Arena show that LoopVLA pushes the efficiency-performance frontier of VLA policies, reducing parameters by 45% and improving inference throughput by up to 1.7 times while matching or outperforming strong baselines in task success.

</details>


### [148] [Learning the Interaction Prior for Protein-Protein Interaction Prediction: A Model-Agnostic Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09964)
*Ziqi Gao,Chenyi Zi,Zijing Liu,Ziqiao Meng,Yu Li,Jia Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出L3-PPI方法，基于生物学L3规则（蛋白质间多条长度为3的路径表明相互作用），通过图提示学习生成带有虚拟L3路径的提示图，将蛋白质对分类转化为图级分类任务，作为即插即用模块增强PPI预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的PPI预测器专注于学习强大的蛋白质表示，但忽视了设计具有生物学意义的分类头，主要依赖拼接或点积等通用聚合方法，缺乏生物学先验信息。

Method: 提出L3-PPI方法，包括：1) 基于蛋白质表示生成带有虚拟L3路径的提示图；2) 通过L3路径正则化控制路径数量；3) 将蛋白质对嵌入的分类重构成对提示图的图级分类任务，作为即插即用模块注入互补性交互先验。

Result: 大量实验表明L3-PPI在多个PPI数据集上取得了优于先进竞争方法的性能提升。

Conclusion: L3-PPI通过引入生物学L3规则先验，有效增强了PPI预测器的性能，验证了将生物学知识融入分类器设计的有效性。

Abstract: Protein-protein interactions (PPIs) are fundamental to cellular function and disease mechanisms. Current learning-based PPI predictors focus on learning powerful protein representations but neglect designing specialized classification heads. They mainly rely on generic aggregating methods like concatenation or dot products, which lack biological insight. Motivated by the biological "L3 rule", where multiple length-3 paths between a pair of proteins indicate their interaction likelihood, our study addresses this gap by designing a biologically informed PPI classifier. In this paper, we provide empirical evidence that popular PPI datasets strongly support the L3 rule. We propose an L3-path-regularized graph prompt learning method called L3-PPI, which can generate a prompt graph with virtual L3 paths based on protein representations and controls the number of paths. L3-PPI reformulates the classification of protein embedding pairs into a graph-level classification task over the generated prompt graph. This lightweight module seamlessly integrates with PPI predictors as a plug-and-play component, injecting the interaction prior of complementarity to enhance performance. Extensive experiments show that L3-PPI achieves superior performance enhancements over advanced competitors.

</details>


### [149] [Prospective Compression in Human Abstraction Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09985)
*Leonardo Hernandez Cano,Ivan Zareski,Luisa El Amouri,Pinzhe Zhao,Max Mascini,Emanuele Sansone,Yewen Pu,Bonan Zhao,Marta Kryven*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究非平稳环境下的程序合成中的在线库学习问题，提出人类在面对非平稳任务分布时，会采用前瞻性压缩策略（针对未来任务）而非回顾性压缩（针对过去任务）。通过在Pattern Builder Task上设计两个实验和六个计算模型，证明人类抽象行为对潜在的非平稳结构敏感，符合前瞻性压缩假说，而现有回顾性压缩算法和LLM模型无法捕捉这一行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有库学习算法基于回顾性压缩，即从静态任务分布中压缩历史任务来构建抽象库。但真实世界的学习环境往往是非平稳的，任务生成过程随时间演变。作者希望探究人类在面对非平稳环境时，是否会选择前瞻性压缩策略——即为了压缩未来任务而构建抽象。

Method: 使用Pattern Builder Task（视觉程序合成范式），让参与者用少量基元、变换和自定义辅助工具构建越来越复杂的几何图案。设计两个实验，采用互补的潜在课程（latent curricula）来区分前瞻性压缩行为与其他库学习策略。同时构建六个计算模型（涵盖在线库学习策略），对比人类行为。

Result: 人类抽象行为对任务生成过程中潜在的非平稳结构敏感，表现出与前瞻性压缩一致的模式。而现有的回顾性压缩算法和基于LLM的程序合成模型无法捕捉这一行为。

Conclusion: 人类在程序合成中的库学习采用前瞻性压缩策略，面向未来任务进行抽象，而非简单地压缩历史任务。现有算法需要改进以纳入这种前瞻性机制，才能更好地模拟人类学习行为。

Abstract: A core challenge in program synthesis is online library learning: the incremental acquisition of reusable abstractions under uncertainty about future task demands. Existing algorithms treat library learning as retrospective compression over a static task distribution, where the learned library is determined by the corpus of past tasks. However, real-world learning domains are often non-stationary, with tasks arising from a generative process that evolves over time. We propose and test the hypothesis that in non-stationary domains human library learning selects abstractions prospectively: targeting compression of future tasks.
